  Attack Steps (per group):
  t=10  ①  Real pair HELLO exchange (V2V link); echo pair also exchange own HELLO
  t=10  ②  All four vehicles send legitimate topology to controller
            (no cross-links between real pair and echo pair)
  t=10  ③  Echo vehicles overhear real-pair HELLO (echo preparation)
  t=10+ ④  Echo vehicles send forged echo reports to controller
  t=10+ ⑤  Controller infers phantom paths via echo vehicles
  t=10+ ⑥  Controller installs faulty routes on phantom paths

========================================================

[t=10.005]  STEP ①  NORMAL V2V TOPOLOGY DISCOVERY
  Measured distances: d(V80,V56)=90.881m  d(V9,V16)=59.067m  (range=300.000m)
  V80 -> V56 : HELLO(Sender=V80, t=10.005116)  (real link pair)
  V56 -> V80 : HELLO(Sender=V56, t=10.005116)  (real link pair)
  V9 -> V16 : HELLO(Sender=V9, t=10.005116)  (echo pair — own legitimate link)
  V16 -> V9 : HELLO(Sender=V16, t=10.005116)  (echo pair — own legitimate link)
  [V9 and V16 are in overhearing range of V80↔V56 but have NO direct link to V80 or V56]

[t=10.005]  STEP ②  LEGITIMATE LINK ESTABLISHMENT
  V80 -> Controller : <V80 sees V56, t=10.005116>  (real reporter)
  V56 -> Controller : <V56 sees V80, t=10.005116>  (real reporter)
  V9 -> Controller : <V9 sees V16, t=10.005116>  (echo pair's own real link)
  V16 -> Controller : <V16 sees V9, t=10.005116>  (echo pair's own real link)
  No direct links: V80-V9, V80-V16, V56-V9, V56-V16 do NOT exist

[t=10.005]  STEP ③  HELLO MESSAGE INTERCEPTION (Echo Preparation)
  V9 overhears HELLO: <V80 sees V56, t=10.005>  [stored for echo]
  V16 overhears HELLO: <V80 sees V56, t=10.005>  [stored for echo]
  Echo vehicles will replay this observation to the controller

[t=10.113]  STEP ④  ECHOED TOPOLOGY OBSERVATION REPORTS
  V18 -> Controller : <V80 sees V56, t=10.005116>  (ECHO -- false reporter)
  V10 -> Controller : <V80 sees V56, t=10.005116>  (ECHO -- false reporter)
  Active false reporters: V18, V10
  V3↔V4 link: dist=93.0m  IN RANGE — Path 4 inferred
  V80↔V56 echoed link: dist=91.3m  IN RANGE — genuinely real
  Note: echo vehicles duplicate an existing link — they do NOT fabricate new physical links

[t=10.1]  STEP ⑤  MULTIPATH INFERENCE AT CONTROLLER (Attack Effect)
  Controller aggregates observations for link V80↔V56:
    (1) V80 reports V80↔V56  [real]
    (2) V56 reports V56↔V80  [real]
    (3) V18 reports V80↔V56  [ECHO]
    (4) V10 reports V80↔V56  [ECHO]
  Controller incorrectly infers:
    Path 1: V80 -> V56  (REAL)
    Path 2: V80 -> V18 -> V56  (PHANTOM)
    Path 3: V80 -> V10 -> V56  (PHANTOM)
    Path 4: V80 -> V18 -> V10 -> V56  (PHANTOM — V3↔V4 in range)

[t=10.1]  STEP ⑥  FAULTY ROUTING DECISIONS
  Controller installs routing rules relying on phantom paths
  Packets to V56 may be forwarded via V18 or V10 (including 3-hop V3→V4 chain) (non-existent paths)
  Expected impact: packet loss, increased delay, routing instability
  <- ATTACK SUCCESS
